Fast objective:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    n=len(a)
    M=2*n-1
    Mfft=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, Mfft)
    b=np.fft.irfft(A*A, Mfft)[:M]
    return b
```
For n=100k, Mfft=262144, okay. Score: 2*n*max(b)/S^2. Use S=1 often.

Let's design core functions:

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def fft_len(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def fast_conv(a): ...
def exact_score(a): ...
def softmax(x): m=x.max(); y=np.exp(x-m); s=y.sum(); if s==0: return np.full(len(x),1/len(x)); return y/s
def lse_obj_x(x, beta):
    p=softmax(x)
    n=len(p)
    c=fast_conv(p)
    cmax=c.max()
    z=beta*(c-cmax)
    # logsumexp stable: max z=0, so logsum = log(exp(z).sum())
    zmax = z.max() # 0
    lse = zmax + np.log(np.exp(z - zmax).sum())
    L = cmax + lse/beta
    val = 2.0*n*L / (1.0) # p sum 1
    # weights
    w = np.exp(z - zmax) / np.exp(z - zmax).sum()
    # grad_p = 2n * 2 * (rev_p * w)[n-1:2n-1]
    r = p[::-1]
    M2 = 3*n - 2? Wait conv of rev_p length n and w length 2n-1 => length 3n-2. Need slice n-1..2n-2 inclusive. Previous M2=3n-2. yes.
    M2_fft = 1 << (M2 -1).bit_length()
    R=np.fft.rfft(r, M2_fft); W=np.fft.rfft(w, M2_fft)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(R*W, M2_fft)[n-1:2n-1]
    grad_p = 4.0*n*conv  # because d score=2n * g where g=2conv
    # Ensure p sum 1 and homogeneous: dot = np.dot(p, grad_p); grad_x = p*(grad_p - dot*p?) Formula: grad_x = p*(grad_p - dot_p*p?) Wait if F scale inv dot(p,grad_p)=0, grad_x_j = p_j*grad_p_j if dot=0. More generally grad_x = p*(grad_p - (p·grad_p)*p?) Let's derive: softmax p. dF/dx_j = ∑ F_i p_i(δij - p_j) = p_j F_j - p_j ∑ F_i p_i = p_j(F_j - dot). Where dot = ∑ p_i F_i. If F is not exactly scale invariant due to numerical? Use that. So grad_x = p*(grad_p - dot*p). Does this maintain zero sum? sum = ∑ p F - dot∑p=0. Good. If dot≈0, same.
    return val, grad_x
```

But objective sum p=1 exactly, F homogeneous? The LSE score as function of p with sum=1; if p changed off simple due to grad? We use softmax, p sum1. Formula with dot okay. If F not homogeneous (due to LSE? It is homogeneous? L = logsumexp(beta(c-cmax))+cmax; c homogeneous degree2, cmax degree2, c-cmax homogeneous? If we extend F(p) = 2n * LSE(c(p)) with no normalization, under scale λ, L scales λ^2, not scale invariant; objective exact uses /S^2 to make invariant. For softmax, S=1 so okay. Gradient for off-simplex not needed except formula uses F_p for F(p)=2n LSE (not normalized). But since we restrict p sum1, actual F is that. Homogeneity not applicable? It is not scale invariant because no /S^2. But p simplex fixed. Formula for grad_x uses F_p as derivatives of F on R^n (not quotient). Since p from softmax, S=1 but F_p also includes variation of sum? We can use general formula with dot, no need homogeneous. For exact objective, we could include /S^2 to make invariant but more complicated. For LSE on simplex, using F=2n LSE with p sum1 is correct; when computing gradient w.r.t x, p sum automatically fixed. The formula grad_x = p*(grad_p - dot(p,grad_p)) works for any F(p) where grad_p = ∂F/∂p_i unconstrained. Here grad_p computed for F=2n LSE (S not divided) = 4n conv. Since p sum=1. Good. This yields correct gradient on simplex. It may have nonzero dot; subtract ensures direction tangent to simplex? Actually softmax already accounts; formula general. Good.

However LSE objective without /S^2 is not flat to global scaling? But softmax fixes scale; gradient formula includes dot term. Fine.

Exact objective and gradient for polish:
Exact score F=2n max(c)/1 (sum=1). Subgradient: active set A where c≈max (within tol). weights w uniform over active. g=2*(rev_p*w)[slice]; grad_p=2n*g. grad_x=p*(grad_p - dot p,p? using F exact? But max not differentiable; subgradient works. For L-BFGS cannot nondiff. Use LSE.

Coordinate descent: For exact objective on simplex, can optimize by moving mass from coordinate i to j. Since score =2n max c with sum=1. def adam_lse_x(x0, beta, lr, t_start, time_limit, max_steps, quiet=False):
```
    x = np.array(x0, dtype=float)
    x -= x.mean()
    n = len(x)
    p = softmax(x)
    m = np.zeros(n); v = np.zeros(n)
    b1,b2=0.9,0.999; eps=1e-8
    best_score_exact = exact_score(p)
    best_x = x.copy()
    # initial LSE val not needed
    for t in range(1,max_steps+1):
       if time.monotonic()-t_start > time_limit: break
       val, grad = lse_obj_x(x, beta)
       # if grad not finite break
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): break
       grad -= grad.mean()
       # optional clamp grad
       maxg = np.max(np.abs(grad))
       if maxg>0: grad /= maxg? This makes step lr in max coordinate; maybe too small for n large. Use rms.
       rm = np.sqrt(np.mean(grad*grad)+1e-12)
       # use Adam or normalized? Let's use Adam but scale grad by 1/rm? If apply Adam to scaled grad, effective step lr; beta ok. Simpler: g = grad / rm; Adam update with lr.
       g = grad / rm
       m = b1*m + (1-b1)*g
       v = b2*v + (1-b2)*g*g
       mhat=m/(1-b1**t); vhat=v/(1-b2**t)
       x = x - lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
       x -= x.mean()
       # maybe clip x to avoid underflow: if range>100? center; if max-min>200, scale? But soft.
       if t % 200 == 0:
          p=softmax(x); sc=exact_score(p)
          if sc<best: update
    final...
```

```
new[j:j+n] += 2δ*p
```

```
Resampling function:
```
def resample_density(a, n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); old_n=len(a); S=a.sum(); if S<=0: return ones/n
   x_old=np.linspace(0.5/(old_n), (old_n-0.5)/old_n, old_n) maybe positions center.
   x_new=np.linspace(0.5/n, (n-0.5)/n, n)
   if old_n==1: val=a[0]/S; return np.full(n,val)
   y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a/S)
   y[np.isnan]=0; y=y/ y.sum()
   return y
```
Using centers avoids edge zero. Alternative linearly index `np.interp(np.linspace(0,n, n,endpoint=False), np.linspace(0,old_n,old_n,endpoint=False), a)`. Both similar. def pair_move_polish(p, c, t_start, time_limit, max_heavy=20000):
```
    n=len(p); best_sc=2*n*max(c)
    # relative weight beta = max(1000, 100/n? Actually if cmax ~1/n, beta = 100/cmax ~100n)

       z=beta*(c-cmax); z=np.minimum(0,z)
       expz=np.exp(z); w=expz/expz.sum()
       # g_L = 2*(rev_p*w)[slice]
       grad = 4*n*(rev_p*w? no: conv_res of rev_p and w, use FFT each pass. Too expensive if many passes. But we can compute c convolution of rev_p and w each pass via FFT O(nlogn).

       improved=False

           # Try fractions maybe decreasing from small to large?

```
Implement `lse_core(x,beta)`:
   x = x - x.mean()
   p=softmax(x)
   n=len
   c=fast_conv(p)
   cmax=float(c.max())
   z=beta*(c-cmax); z = np.minimum(0,z)
   expz=np.exp(z); ws=expz.sum(); w=expz/ws
   L=cmax + float(np.log(ws))/beta
   F=2*n*L
   rev=p[::-1]
   M2=...; R,W; conv_res=irfft(...)[n-1:2n-1]
   gL=2*conv
   grad_x=p*(2*n*(gL - 2*L))
   # enforce finite, mean zero
   grad_x -= grad_x.mean()
   return F, grad_x, cmax
```

```
def adam_lse_x(x0, beta, lr, t_start, time_limit, max_steps=None, check_interval=50):
    x=x0.copy(); x-=x.mean()
    n=len(x)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); b1,b2=0.9,0.999; eps=1e-8
    best_x=x.copy(); best_score=inf
    last_exact=inf
    step=0
    t1=b1**step ... update each iteration maybe use formulas.
    while ...:
       F, grad, cmax = lse_core(x,beta)
       if not finite: break
       sc=2*n*cmax
       if sc<best_score: best_score=sc; best_x=x.copy()
       if time over break
       # normalize grad
       grad-=grad.mean()
       gm=np.sqrt(np.mean(grad*grad)+1e-30)
       g=grad/(gm+eps)
       m = b1*m+(1-b1)*g
       v = b2*v+(1-b2)*g*g
       mhat=m/(1-b1**step)
       vhat=v/(1-b2**step)
       x = x - lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
       x -= x.mean()
       # optional magnitude clip to 100? If x max-min>100, center and maybe scale? Let's clip to [-80,80] to avoid underflow but may bias. Use no clip, but if np.nan break.
       step +=1
    final exact compute? Already track.
    return best_score,best_x
```

```
def run_lbfgs_x(x0, beta, t_start, time_limit):
    remaining = time_limit - (time.monotonic()-t_start)
    if remaining<2: return ...
    # measure one eval
    tmeas=time.monotonic(); lse_core(x0,beta); eval_time=max(1e-4, time.monotonic()-tmeas)
    maxfun=int(min(3000, max(50, (remaining*0.8)/eval_time/3.0))) # 3 safety due line search

    xres=res.x; p=softmax(xres); sc=exact_score(p)
```

`fast_conv`:
```
def fast_conv(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    out_len=2*n-1
    nfft=_next_pow2(out_len)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft)
    B=np.fft.irfft(A*A,nfft)[:out_len]
    # numerical negative tiny? For nonnegative conv should nonneg but FFT errors. Clip? max unaffected; negative tiny might affect? Use `B = np.maximum(B,0.0)`? But if rounding negative, for gradient weights okay. Clipping improves. Do `B[B<0 & >-1e-15]=0`? Simpler `np.clip(B,0,None)`; but can create tiny positive. Since p nonnegative, true conv nonnegative. Clipping all negatives to 0 safe? It may slightly alter objective (increase? setting negative to 0 increases if negative? Actually negative due to rround; max unaffected if small. Could hide structure but okay.) Use `B = np.where(B < 0.0, 0.0, B)`? For gradient active weights, if a coefficient should be zero but negative clamped 0 fine.
    return B
```
Clipping could make sum c slightly >? Not important. Use `B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)`.

`exact_score`:
```
def exact_score(a):
    n=len(a)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-12: return np.inf
    c=fast_conv(a)
    m=c.max()
    return float(2.0*n*m/(S*S))
```
If p sum1, `score_from_cmax = 2*n*cmax`.

`normalize_nonnegative`:
```
def normalize_nonnegative(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    S=a.sum()
    if S <=1e-12:
       return np.ones(len(a), dtype=np.float64)/len(a) if len(a)>0 else np.ones(1)
    return a/S
```
If inf values, set 0. Length? if empty return [1].

`softmax`:
```
def softmax_x(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    m=np.max(x)
    if not np.isfinite(m): ...
    y=np.exp(x-m)
    s=y.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite:
       y=np.zeros_like(x); y[np.argmax(x)]=1.0; return y
    return y/s
```
If x has NaN, replace? Return uniform? We can in optimizer detect.

`lse_core` with high beta: Need ensure beta>0. If beta*range huge >700, `np.exp(z)` underflow okay. `z` maybe -1000; exp 0. `ws.sum()` at least 1. `L = cmax + log(ws)/beta`. If ws zero due all underflow? But z includes cmax exactly 0 for at least one max, exp 0=1; unless cmax NaN. Good.
Gradient convolution: If n=1? Sequence length at least 2? Could be 1; handle. For n=1: out_len=1, c=[p0^2], cmax=1 (sum1), L=1, F=2. grad? formula slice n-1..2n-1 =0..1? M2=1? revp length1, w length1, conv length1; gL=2? g=2conv? Need correct. But optimize n large. Add simple if n==1: return 2.0, [0.0], 1.0.

`M2 = 3*n - 2`; `M2_fft=_next_pow2(M2)`; `conv_res=np.fft.irfft(RA*W, M2_fft)[n-1:2*n-1]`. Need verify length exactly n. `R*W` complex length M2_fft/2+1; irfft returns M2_fft; slice valid.

`np.fft.rftf` of rev_p: rev_p is negative stride? `p[::-1]` view; rfft okay. Could copy? not needed.

`lse_obj_x`:
```
def lse_obj_x(x, beta, scale):
    if beta is None: beta=...
    F,grad,cmax=lse_core(x,beta)
    if not np.isfinite(F): return np.inf, np.zeros_like(x)
    if scale !=1.0:
        return scale*F, scale*grad
    return F, grad
```
Minimize requires scalar, gradient same shape. If `scale` array? float.

`adam_lse_x`: Use `deadline` absolute. `max_steps` optional. Need not recompute x0? We'll use `x0` center at start.
```
def adam_lse_x(x0, beta, lr, deadline, max_steps=None):
    n=len(x0)
    x=x0.astype(float).copy(); x-=x.mean()
    m=zeros; v=zeros; step=0
    best_score=np.inf; best_x=x.copy()
    # initial eval? We'll eval in loop.
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and (max_steps is None or step<max_steps):
       F, grad, cmax = lse_core(x,beta)
       if not finite: break
       sc=2*n*cmax
       if sc<best_score:
           best_score=sc; best_x=x.copy()
       step +=1
       # grad normalize
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): break
       grad -= grad.mean()
       rm = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(grad*grad)))
       if not finite or rm <1e-300: break
       g=grad/rm
       m = 0.9*m +0.1*g
       v =0.999*v+0.001*g*g
       b1p=0.9**step; b2p=0.999**step
       mhat=m/(1-b1p); vhat=v/(1-b2p)
       x = x - lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
       # center, clip if extreme
       x-=x.mean()
       # if x range > 200? scale maybe:
       rng = np.max(x)-np.min(x)
       if not np.isfinite(rng) or rng > 200.0:
           # reduce large excursions by scaling deviations toward mean
           x = x.mean() + (x-x.mean())*0.9
       # NaN check
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): break
    # final eval maybe to ensure best
    return best_score, best_x
```
Initial x from log; if best_score remains inf? Should eval. Good.
`lr` could be 0; no.
The range scaling if >200 modifies x towards mean, but objective changes; may be okay to prevent underflow. Maybe harmful. Could omit. We'll do if >100: `x = x - x.mean(); x = x * (100.0/rng)` to clip range. This projects ray; could move away optimizer. Better just clip component-wise? `x = np.clip(x, x.mean()-80, x.mean()+80)`? If one coordinate dominates, clipping may affect. But x range in log p; 80 corresponds p ratio e^80~1e34, enough. Use `if rng > 80: center; scale =80/rng; x = x*max(0, scale)`. This maintains relative ranking, just compresses extremes. Could affect but avoids underflow. Maybe not needed. If we added floor, min x ~ -27; optimizer may push more negative. Use maybe 100. Fine.

But with normalized grad and Adam, x range may not explode. We can skip scaling for simplicity; if range >150, center and `x = np.clip(x, -150,150)` after center. Since softmax with max shift, differences >150 effectively zero; clipping okay. Use `if rng > 150: xc=x-x.mean(); x = np.clip(xc, -150,150)`.

`exact_score_from_x`: `p=softmax_x(x); exact_score(p)`. Use after L-BFGS.

`run_lbfgs_x`:
```
def run_lbfgs_x(x0, beta, deadline, maxfun_cap=800):
   n=len(x0)
   base_p=softmax_x(x0); base_score=exact_score(base_p)
   if time.monotonic() > deadline - 1: return base_score, x0
   # measure one core
   t=time.monotonic(); lse_core(x0,beta); eval_time=max(1e-4, time.monotonic()-t)
   # maxfun: conservative in line search, cap based on n
   remaining=deadline-time.monotonic()
   # line search might do multiple f evals per iter; estimate 5 per line? Use 10 safety.
   est = int(max(30, min(maxfun_cap, remaining*0.5/eval_time/5.0)))
   scale=float(n) # consistent scaling
   try:
      res = minimize(lse_obj_x, x0, args=(float(beta), float(scale)), jac=True,
                     options={'maxiter': max(30, est*2), 'maxfun': est, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-9, 'maxls':50})
      xres=np.asarray(res.x,float)
   except Exception as e:
      print(...); return base_score, x0
   xres = np.where(np.isfinite(xres), xres, x0)
   xres -= xres.mean()
   p=softmax_x(xres); sc=exact_score(p)
   if not finite or sc>base_score maybe still return xres? We can return xres but local best handles.
   return sc, xres
```
Issue: `minimize` may not respect deadline if est too high. We estimate eval_time but line search might call many; maxfun caps function eval, each eval one core. But it may do many iterations without f? no. maxfun=est ensures at most est f evals. Linear search function evals count? In L-BFGS-B, maxfun is max function evaluations; line search also uses f evals? I think yes `nfev`. So time ~est*eval_time. Good. Use safety factor 2 maybe: `est = remaining*0.5/eval_time/2`? If line search multiple f per iteration included in nfev, yes. But `minimize` may call f for gradient? jac. We'll use `est = int(max(20, min(cap, remaining*0.6/eval_time/2.0)))`, cap 1500. For n=8192 eval_time few ms, remaining 100s ->30000 cap 1500. L-BFGS 1500 f evals maybe 15s. Good. For n=100k eval_time0.02 -> remaining 100 ->3000/2=1500 cap. Good. 1500*0.02=30s. Good.
Could be too low to converge; but multiple beta.

`pair_move_polish`:
Need be efficient and safe. Let's implement with in-place temporary? To avoid allocating for each candidate, maybe use copy. Simpler but memory. We'll write:
```
def pair_move_polish(p, c, deadline, max_evals=5000):
    n=len(p)
    # p normalized, c exact conv
    c = c.copy(); p = p.copy()
    best_score=2*n*float(np.max(c))
    eps=1e-18
    evaluations=0
    # precompute fracs
    fracs=[0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.7,1.0]
    K = 16 if n<=8192 else (10 if n<=32768 else 6)
    max_pairs_evals = 3 if n<=8192 else (2 if n<=32768 else 1)? Actually each pair tries multiple fracs; count each frac eval.
    # Use while loop reweight maybe 5 times
    for _pass in range(5):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       cmax=float(np.max(c))
       M=max(cmax,1e-300)
       beta=max(1000.0, 50.0/M)  # beta*cmax~50
       z=beta*(c-cmax); z=np.minimum(0,z); ez=np.exp(z); ws=ez.sum(); w=ez/ws
       # gradient g_L = 2*(...); But for pair selection we can use unnormalized conv (without 2n).
       r=p[::-1]
       M2=3*n-2; M2fft=_next_pow2(M2)
       conv_res=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(r,M2fft)*np.fft.rfft(w,M2fft), M2fft)[n-1:2*n-1]
       g=2.0*conv_res
       # sources high g, destinations low g
       # Restrict sources p>1e-12
       p_safe=np.where(p>1e-15, g, -np.inf)
       src=np.argsort(p_safe)[-K:]
       dst=np.argsort(g)[:K]
       # build candidate pairs unique, potential = (g_src - g_dst)*p_src
       pairs=set? We can create list with sorting.
       candidates=[]
       for i in src:
         if p[i]<=1e-15: continue
         for j in dst:
            if i==j: continue
            pot=(g[i]-g[j])*p[i]
            if pot>1e-30: candidates.append((pot,i,j))
       candidates.sort(reverse=True)
       improved_any=False
       # iterate top candidates up to pair_limit
       pair_limit = 200 if n<=8192 else (80 if n<=32768 else 30)
       for pot,i,j in candidates[:pair_limit]:
          if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
          if evaluations>max_evals: break
          max_delta = p[i]
          best_delta=0; best_new_c=None; best_new_max=np.inf
          for frac in fracs:
             delta=frac*max_delta
             if delta <= 1e-18: continue
             # update copy
             new_c = c.copy()
             coeff=2.0*delta
             new_c[j:j+n] += coeff*p
             new_c[i:i+n] -= coeff*p
             delta2=delta*delta
             new_c[2*j] += delta2
             new_c[i+j] -= 2.0*delta2
             new_c[2*i] += delta2
             new_max=float(np.max(new_c))
             evaluations +=1
             if new_max < best_new_max:
                best_new_max=new_max; best_delta=delta; best_new_c=new_c
          if best_delta>0 and best_new_max < cmax - 1e-15:
              c=best_new_c; p[i] -= best_delta; p[j] += best_delta
              # clean
              if p[i] < 1e-15: p[i]=0.0
              cmax=best_new_max
              improved_any=True
              # periodically renormalize? sum constant; if p[j]>1000? sum1 so no.
              if evaluations>max_evals: break
       if not improved_any: break
       # after accepted moves, update best_score
       sc=2*n*cmax
       if sc<best_score: best_score=sc
       # maybe renormalize p if drift
       S=p.sum(); if abs(S-1)>1e-9: p/=S; c=fast_conv(p)
    final_sc=2*n*float(np.max(c))
    # normalize output p sum maybe
    p=np.clip(p,0,None); S=p.sum(); if S>0: p/=S
    return final_sc,p
```
Critical: In move update, `new_c[j:j+n] += coeff*p` and `new_c[i:i+n] -= coeff*p`. If i and j slices overlap, in-place sequential operations with `coeff*p` vector p are okay because source p unchanged. But when slices overlap, adding and subtracting over overlapping regions order matters? Addition operations are linear; if we do in-place, e.g., new_c[k] receives both + and -; operations commute, and reading p unaffected. Fine. But note `new_c[j:j+n] += coeff*p` uses `coeff*p` allocated new array; for large n each candidate alloc, okay.
Quadratic indices: `2*j`, `i+j`, `2*i` are within [0,2n-2]. Since i,j < n. good.
But if `i` or `j` >? loop. good.
Potential: The cross term formula with p old includes order; if i and j slice same? We skip.
Acceptance criterion: `new_max < cmax - 1e-15` maybe too strict; use `< cmax - 1e-14 * max(1,cmax)`? cmax ~1e-5, difference could be 1e-8. Use `tol = 1e-12 * max(1.0, cmax)`. If new_max < cmax - tol.
Since FFT c may have small negative/round; clamped. Pair update exact based on c, but initial c maybe clipped; accept if max lower. Good.
After pair moves, c may have tiny negative due float? Moves exact on nonnegative p should keep nonnegative, but numerical small; we can `new_c=np.maximum(new_c,0)` before max? That may hide accuracy but okay. Add to candidate: `new_c = np.maximum(new_c,0.0)`? This adds O(n). Maybe not needed; can after update `if np.any(new_c< -1e-12) do recompute?` Simpler not.

Pair polish using c from fast_conv; if not passed, recompute.

Advantages: exact local improvement. But if pair moves slow, ensure not consume too much deadline. Set `max_evals` perhaps 5000 for n=8192, 2000 for 32768, 500 for 100k. Each eval copies c (2n). For n=100k 500*200k=100M memory ops, okay 1s? fine.

We can use pair polish on current best p for all n. Need pass c. To avoid recompute, compute c=fast_conv(p). Time includes FFT.

`resample_density`:
```
def resample_density(a, n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    S=a.sum(); if S<=0...
    old_n=len(a)
    if old_n==n: return a/S
    if old_n==1: return np.full(n, a[0]/S)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,old_n) # endpoints?
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a/S)
    y=np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y=np.maximum(y,0.0)
    t=y.sum(); if t<=0: return ones/n; return y/t
```
End vs centers. Which? For density as piecewise constant over intervals, center interpolation avoids endpoint value influence. Use centers:
```
if old_n>1:
   x_old=(np.arange(old_n)+0.5)/old_n
else...
   x_new=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
```
This maps midpoints. Good. If old sequence has features at boundaries, interpolation not include exact endpoint, but boundary negligible. Use centers. For n=2, x_new [0.25,0.75].

Another resample variant with endpoints could be included? Maybe center enough.

Smooth sequence:
```
def smooth_sequence(a, kernel=None):
   n=len(a); if n<3 return normalize
   if kernel is None: kernel=np.array([1,2,3,2,1],float)/9
   # if n < len(kernel): kernel=np.ones(n)/n
   b=np.convolve(a, kernel, mode='same')
   b=np.maximum(b,0); S=b.sum(); if S>0: b/=S; return b
```
`np.convolve` direct O(n*k) small, k5.

```
     screen_results initial
     for ... run adam until min(screen_dl, now+each)
     sort
     adam_dl = start + min(0.7*total, max(60, 0.7*total?)) simply start+0.7*total, cap 1200.
     # extension top until adam_dl (remaining after screen)

        rem=adam_dl-now; top_alloc=rem*0.65; d=now+top_alloc

        if second and now<adam_dl: alloc=adam_dl-now; d=now+alloc; run
```

`simple_sequence` kinds: Need no issues. Implement:
```
def simple_sequence(n, kind):
    x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    if kind == 'uniform': a=np.ones(n)
    elif kind == 'lin_inc': a=x
    elif kind == 'lin_dec': a=1.0-x
    elif kind == 'lin_asym': a=0.2 + x + np.sin(2*np.pi*x)*0.2? Ensure nonneg: 0.3 + x.
    elif kind == 'quad_bowl': a=(x-0.3)**2 + 0.1
    elif kind == 'quad_peak': a=1.0 - 0.8*(x-0.7)**2
    elif kind == 'gauss_left': a=np.exp(-0.5*((x-0.3)/0.15)**2)+0.01
    elif kind == 'gauss_right': a=np.exp(-0.5*((x-0.7)/0.15)**2)+0.01
    elif kind == 'two_asym': a=np.exp(-0.5*((x-0.25)/0.12)**2) + 0.5*np.exp(-0.5*((x-0.75)/0.1)**2)+0.01
    else: a=np.ones(n)
    a=np.maximum(a,1e-6); a/=a.sum(); return a
```
This is okay.

```
    if len(a)<16: return
    sc=exact_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return

```

Because F=2nL, grad_un for F? Wait earlier: grad_un of F=2nL wrt p = 2n * gL. grad_x for F on softmax = p*(grad_un - dot(grad_un,p)). Since L homogeneous degree2, dot(grad_un,p) = 2n*2L =4nL. Thus grad_x = p*(2n gL -4nL) = 2n p*(gL -2L). Correct.

`optimize_candidate` code flow:
```
def optimize_candidate(p, deadline):
  start=time.monotonic()
  total=deadline-start
  p=normalize_nonnegative(p)
  best_score=exact_score(p); best_p=p.copy()
  p_pos=np.maximum(p,1e-12); p_pos/=p_pos.sum(); x=np.log(p_pos); best_x=x.copy()
  n=len(p)
  if total > 30:
    screen_dl = start + max(5.0, min(40.0, 0.08*total))
    screen_results=[]
    init_factor=20; init_lr=0.05
    screen_results.append((best_score, x.copy(), int(min(1e8,max(1000,init_factor*n))), init_lr))
    if total>60:
       grid = ADAM_GRID
       n_grid=len(grid)
       screen_each = max(0.5, (screen_dl - time.monotonic())/n_grid)
       for bf,lr in grid:
           d = min(screen_dl, time.monotonic()+screen_each)
           if time.monotonic() >= d: break
           beta=int(min(1e8,max(1000,bf*float(n))))
           sc,xr = adam_lse_x(x, beta, lr, d, max_steps=20000)
           screen_results.append((sc, xr, beta, lr))
       screen_results.sort()
       adam_dl = start + min(0.70*total, 1500.0)
       if time.monotonic() < adam_dl and screen_results:
          top=screen_results[0]
          rem=adam_dl-time.monotonic()
          if rem>5:
             d=time.monotonic()+max(5.0, rem*0.65)
             sc,xr=adam_lse_x(top[1], top[2], top[3], d)
             p_new=softmax_x(xr); sc_new=exact_score(p_new)
             if sc_new<best_score: update
          if time.monotonic()<adam_dl and len(screen_results)>1:
             second=screen_results[1]
             d=adam_dl
             sc,xr=adam_lse_x(second[1], second[2], second[3], d)
             update
       # L-BFGS
       lbfgs_dl = start + 0.93*total
       for i,fac in enumerate(LBFGS_FACTORS):
          if time.monotonic() >= lbfgs_dl - 3: break
          rem = lbfgs_dl - time.monotonic()
          if rem < 10: break
          if len(LBFGS_FACTORS)-i == 1: alloc = rem*0.9
          elif len... ==2: alloc = rem*0.55
          else: alloc = rem*0.4
          d = time.monotonic() + max(5.0, alloc)
          beta=int(min(1e8,max(1000,fac*float(n))))
          sc,xr=run_lbfgs_x(best_x,beta,d)
          p_new=softmax_x(xr); sc_new=exact_score(p_new)
          if sc_new<best_score: update
          # even if sc_new not better, maybe if sc_new < best_score+1e-4, keep? no
       # Pair
       if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
          c=fast_conv(best_p)
          sc_new, p_new=pair_move_polish(best_p, c, deadline)
          if sc_new<best_score: update
  # final ensure best_p normalized
  return best_score,best_p
```
If best_x from L-BFGS not updated because score worse, but lower beta L-BFGS may start from best_x; okay. If L-BFGS worsens but maybe new x basin on next higher beta? Not. Could still return xres with score within 1e-5 and use? No.

Potential bug: In L-BFGS allocation, if first beta takes `rem*0.4`, second takes remaining? The loop updates rem each time; for 3 factors, first 40%, second 55% of remaining (24%), third 90% of remaining (about13%). Good.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    score(a) = 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2
using multi-scale interpolation from the provided construction,
LSE-smoothed gradient descent in log-space, L-BFGS polishing, and exact
two-coordinate/local max-convolution moves.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

MAX_N = 20000

ADAM_GRID = [
    (5, 0.02),
    (5, 0.10),
    (20, 0.02),
    (20, 0.08),
    (100, 0.01),
    (100, 0.04),
    (400, 0.01),
]

LBFGS_FACTORS = [20, 100, 400]
PAIR_PASS_BETAS = [50.0, 20.0, 5.0, 100.0]


def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = _next_pow2(out_len)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    B = np.fft.irfft(A * A, nfft)[:out_len]
    B = np.where(B > 0.0, B, 0.0)
    return B


def exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_conv(a)
    m = float(c.max())
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (S * S))


def normalize_nonnegative(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.where(a > 0.0, a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / S


def resample_density(a, n):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    old_n = len(a)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if old_n == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if old_n == n:
        return a
    if old_n == 1:
        return np.full(n, a[0] / float(a.sum()), dtype=np.float64)

    x_old = (np.arange(old_n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(old_n)
    x_new = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a / float(a.sum()))
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y = np.where(y > 0.0, y, 0.0)
    S = float(y.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return y / S


def smooth_sequence(a):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    n = len(a)
    if n < 3:
        return a
    if n < 5:
        kern = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        kern = np.array([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 2.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        kern /= float(kern.sum())
    b = np.convolve(a, kern, mode="same")
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.where(b > 0.0, b, 0.0)
    S = float(b.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return a
    return b / S


def simple_sequence(n, kind):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    if kind == "uniform":
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif kind == "lin_inc":
        a = 0.25 + x
    elif kind == "lin_dec":
        a = 0.25 + (1.0 - x)
    elif kind == "quad_bowl":
        a = 0.25 + (x - 0.35) * (x - 0.35)
    elif kind == "quad_peak":
        a = 0.25 + (1.0 - 0.9 * (x - 0.65) * (x - 0.65))
    elif kind == "gauss_left":
        a = 0.05 + np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - 0.28) / 0.16) ** 2)
    elif kind == "gauss_right":
        a = 0.05 + np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - 0.72) / 0.13) ** 2)
    elif kind == "two_asym":
        a = (
            0.05
            + np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - 0.27) / 0.12) ** 2)
            + 0.45 * np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - 0.73) / 0.10) ** 2)
        )
    else:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.where(a > 1e-8, a, 1e-8)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / S


def add_candidate(arrays, infos, a):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if len(a) < 16:
        return
    sc = exact_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    idx = len(arrays)
    arrays.append(a)
    infos.append((float(sc), int(idx), int(len(a))))


def generate_candidates(parent, deadline):
    arrays = []
    infos = []

    add_candidate(arrays, infos, parent)

    lengths = []
    pl = len(parent)
    if 1024 <= pl <= MAX_N:
        lengths.append(pl)
    for nn in [4096, 8192, 16384, MAX_N]:
        duplicate = False
        for mm in lengths:
            if abs(mm - nn) <= 256:
                duplicate = True
                break
        if not duplicate:
            lengths.append(nn)
    lengths = sorted(set([int(x) for x in lengths if 1024 <= x <= MAX_N]))[:6]

    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        a = resample_density(parent, n)
        add_candidate(arrays, infos, a)

        a2 = a
        if n > 1:
            a2 = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
        add_candidate(arrays, infos, a2)

        a3 = smooth_sequence(a)
        add_candidate(arrays, infos, a3)

        if n > 1:
            a4 = smooth_sequence(a2)
            add_candidate(arrays, infos, a4)

    for n in [4096, 16384, MAX_N]:
        for kind in ["uniform", "lin_inc", "lin_dec", "quad_bowl", "gauss_left", "gauss_right", "two_asym"]:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            a = simple_sequence(n, kind)
            add_candidate(arrays, infos, a)

    if len(arrays) == 0:
        add_candidate(arrays, infos, parent)

    infos.sort()
    return arrays, infos


def softmax_x(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(x))
    y = np.exp(x - m)
    s = float(y.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        y = np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
        y[int(np.argmax(x))] = 1.0
        return y
    return y / s


def lse_core(x, beta):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.array([], dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        beta = 1.0

    x = x - float(np.mean(x))
    p = softmax_x(x)
    S = float(p.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        S = 1.0
    else:
        p = p / S

    c = fast_conv(p)
    cmax = float(c.max())

    z = beta * (c - cmax)
    z = np.where(z < 0.0, z, 0.0)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    ws = float(ez.sum())
    if ws <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = ez / ws

    L = cmax + float(np.log(max(ws, 1e-300))) / beta
    F = float(2.0 * n * L / (S * S))

    rev = p[::-1]
    M2 = 3 * n - 2
    nfft2 = _next_pow2(M2)
    RA = np.fft.rfft(rev, nfft2)
    WA = np.fft.rfft(w, nfft2)
    conv_res = np.fft.irfft(RA * WA, nfft2)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    gL = 2.0 * conv_res

    # Gradient of the normalized LSE score w.r.t. log-softmax coordinates.
    grad_x = p * (2.0 * n * (gL - 2.0 * L))
    grad_x = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_x), grad_x, 0.0)
    grad_x = grad_x - float(np.mean(grad_x))

    return F, grad_x, cmax


def lse_obj_x(x, beta, scale):
    F, grad_x, _ = lse_core(x, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(F):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(np.asarray(x).shape, dtype=np.float64)
    if scale != 1.0:
        return float(scale) * F, float(scale) * grad_x
    return F, grad_x


def adam_lse_x(x0, beta, lr, deadline, max_steps=None):
    n = int(np.asarray(x0).size)
    x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    x = x - float(np.mean(x))

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-9

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_x = x.copy()

    lr_cur = float(lr)
    max_lr = max(1.0, 10.0 * float(lr))
    min_lr = max(1e-6, 0.05 * float(lr))

    step = 0
    last_improve_step = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if max_steps is not None and step >= max_steps:
            break

        F, grad_x, cmax = lse_core(x, beta)
        if not np.isfinite(F):
            break

        sc = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_x = x.copy()
            last_improve_step = step
            lr_cur = min(max_lr, lr_cur * 1.04)
        elif step - last_improve_step > 300:
            lr_cur = max(min_lr, lr_cur * 0.82)

        step += 1
        if step > 2000000:
            break

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_x)):
            break
        grad_x = grad_x - float(np.mean(grad_x))
        rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(grad_x * grad_x)))
        if rms <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(rms):
            break

        g = grad_x / rms
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * g
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * g * g

        b1_t = b1 ** step
        b2_t = b2 ** step
        m_hat = m / (1.0 - b1_t)
        v_hat = v / (1.0 - b2_t)

        x = x - lr_cur * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + eps)
        x = x - float(np.mean(x))

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            break

        rng = float(np.max(x) - np.min(x))
        if not np.isfinite(rng) or rng > 180.0:
            xc = x - float(np.mean(x))
            if rng > 0:
                xc = xc * (150.0 / rng)
            x = xc

    return best_score, best_x


def run_lbfgs_x(x0, beta, deadline):
    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = x0.size
    base_score = exact_score(softmax_x(x0))
    base_x = x0.copy()

    if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1.0:
        return base_score, base_x

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    lse_core(x0, float(beta))
    eval_time = max(1e-4, time.monotonic() - t0)

    remaining = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())
    if remaining <= 2.0:
        return base_score, base_x

    cap = 2500 if n <= 25000 else 1000
    est = int(max(20, min(cap, (remaining * 0.8) / max(eval_time, 1e-4) / 4.0)))
    est = min(est, 6000)

    scale = float(n)
    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_obj_x,
            x0,
            args=(float(beta), scale),
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxiter": max(50, 2 * est),
                "maxfun": est,
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-9,
                "maxls": 30,
            },
        )
        xres = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        xres = base_x

    xres = np.where(np.isfinite(xres), xres, base_x)
    xres = xres - float(np.mean(xres))

    sc = exact_score(softmax_x(xres))
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return base_score, base_x
    return sc, xres


def pair_move_polish(p, c, deadline):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = p.size

    S = float(p.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return exact_score(p), p

    p = np.where(p > 0.0, p, 0.0)
    p = p / S
    c = fast_conv(p)

    best_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
    evaluations = 0

    if n <= 8192:
        K = 18
        pair_limit = 260
        max_evals = 5000
    elif n <= 20000:
        K = 14
        pair_limit = 140
        max_evals = 2600
    else:
        K = 8
        pair_limit = 50
        max_evals = 800

    fracs = [0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.60, 0.90]
    accepted_in_pass = 0

    for pass_factor in PAIR_PASS_BETAS:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2.0:
            break
        if evaluations >= max_evals:
            break

        cmax = float(c.max())
        M = max(cmax, 1e-300)
        beta = max(1000.0, float(pass_factor) / M)

        z = beta * (c - cmax)
        z = np.where(z < 0.0, z, 0.0)
        ez = np.exp(z)
        ws = float(ez.sum())
        if ws <= 1e-300:
            w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = ez / ws

        rev = p[::-1]
        M2 = 3 * n - 2
        nfft = _next_pow2(M2)
        conv_res = np.fft.irfft(
            np.fft.rfft(rev, nfft) * np.fft.rfft(w, nfft),
            nfft,
        )[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
        g = 2.0 * conv_res

        src_scores = np.where(p > 1e-14, g, -np.inf)
        src = np.argsort(src_scores)[-K:]
        dst = np.argsort(g)[:K]

        candidates = []
        for i in src:
            i = int(i)
            if p[i] <= 1e-14:
                continue
            for j in dst:
                j = int(j)
                if i == j:
                    continue
                pot = float((g[i] - g[j]) * p[i])
                if pot > 1e-30:
                    candidates.append((pot, i, j))

        candidates.sort(key=lambda t: t[0], reverse=True)  # no lambda? Let's replace? Actually Python lambda is a lambda function. Rule says no lambda. Need remove.
```

```
        candidates.reverse()

        improved = False
        for _, i, j in candidates[:pair_limit]:

            i = int(i)
            j = int(j)
            if i == j or p[i] <= 1e-16:
                continue

            cmax = float(c.max())
            tol = 1e-12 * max(1.0, cmax)

            best_delta = 0.0
            best_new_c = None
            best_new_max = float("inf")

            for frac in fracs:
                delta = float(frac) * float(p[i])
                if delta <= 1e-18:
                    continue

                new_c = c.copy()
                coeff = 2.0 * delta
                new_c[j:j + n] += coeff * p
                new_c[i:i + n] -= coeff * p
                delta2 = delta * delta
                new_c[2 * j] += delta2
                new_c[i + j] -= 2.0 * delta2
                new_c[2 * i] += delta2

                evaluations += 1
                nm = float(new_c.max())
                if nm < best_new_max:
                    best_new_max = nm
                    best_delta = delta
                    best_new_c = new_c

            if best_delta > 0.0 and best_new_max < cmax - tol:
                c = best_new_c
                p[i] -= best_delta
                p[j] += best_delta
                if p[i] < 1e-15:
                    p[i] = 0.0
                if p[j] < 0.0:
                    p[j] = 0.0
                cmax = best_new_max
                improved = True
                accepted_in_pass += 1

                if accepted_in_pass % 80 == 0:
                    p = np.where(p > 0.0, p, 0.0)
                    S2 = float(p.sum())
                    if S2 > 1e-15:
                        p = p / S2
                    c = fast_conv(p)
                    cmax = float(c.max())

        if not improved:
            # try next beta factor; if all fail, final loop ends.
            accepted_in_pass = 0

    p = np.where(p > 0.0, p, 0.0)
    S = float(p.sum())
    if S > 1e-15:
        p = p / S
    c = fast_conv(p)
    final_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max()) / (S * S) if S > 1e-15 else float("inf")
    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        final_score = exact_score(p)
    return final_score, p


def optimize_candidate(p, deadline):
    start = time.monotonic()
    p = normalize_nonnegative(p)
    n = len(p)

    best_score = exact_score(p)
    best_p = p.copy()

    p_pos = np.where(p > floor, p, floor)
    p_pos = p_pos / float(p_pos.sum())
    x = np.log(p_pos)
    best_x = x.copy()

    def update_from_x(x_new, score_hint):
        nonlocal best_score, best_p, best_x
        p_new = softmax_x(x_new)
        sc = exact_score(p_new)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            return
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p_new.copy()
            best_x = x_new.copy()
```

```python
"""
Multi-scale search for non-negative sequences minimizing
score(a) = 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The program first resamples the provided/state-of-the-art sequence to several
lengths and creates smoother/symmetric transformations. Then it optimizes the
best candidates using:
  - LSE-smoothed maximum-convolution objective,
  - Adam-style updates in log-space (softmax parameterization),
  - L-BFGS polishing with increasing LSE sharpness,
  - exact two-coordinate "mass-move" local minimization.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

MAX_N = 20000

ADAM_GRID = [
    (5, 0.02),
    (5, 0.10),
    (20, 0.02),
    (20, 0.08),
    (100, 0.01),
    (100, 0.04),
    (400, 0.01),
]

LBFGS_FACTORS = [20, 100, 400]
PAIR_PASS_BETAS = [50.0, 20.0, 5.0, 100.0]


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def fast_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = _next_pow2(out_len)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    B = np.fft.irfft(A * A, nfft)[:out_len]
    B = np.where(B > 0.0, B, 0.0)
    return B


def exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_conv(a)
    m = float(c.max())
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (S * S))


def normalize_nonnegative(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.where(a > 0.0, a, 0.0)
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / S


def resample_density(a, n):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    old_n = len(a)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if old_n == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if old_n == n:
        return a
    if old_n == 1:
        return np.full(n, a[0] / float(a.sum()), dtype=np.float64)

    x_old = (np.arange(old_n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(old_n)
    x_new = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a / float(a.sum()))
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y = np.where(y > 0.0, y, 0.0)
    S = float(y.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return y / S


def smooth_sequence(a):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    n = len(a)
    if n < 3:
        return a
    if n < 5:
        kern = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        kern = np.array([1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 2.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        kern /= float(kern.sum())
    b = np.convolve(a, kern, mode="same")
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.where(b > 0.0, b, 0.0)
    S = float(b.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return a
    return b / S


def simple_sequence(n, kind):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    if kind == "uniform":
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif kind == "lin_inc":
        a = 0.25 + x
    elif kind == "lin_dec":
        a = 0.25 + (1.0 - x)
    elif kind == "quad_bowl":
        a = 0.25 + (x - 0.35) * (x - 0.35)
    elif kind == "quad_peak":
        a = 0.25 + (1.0 - 0.9 * (x - 0.65) * (x - 0.65))
    elif kind == "gauss_left":
        a = 0.05 + np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - 0.28) / 0.16) ** 2)
    elif kind == "gauss_right":
        a = 0.05 + np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - 0.72) / 0.13) ** 2)
    elif kind == "two_asym":
        a = (
            0.05
            + np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - 0.27) / 0.12) ** 2)
            + 0.45 * np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - 0.73) / 0.10) ** 2)
        )
    else:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.where(a > 1e-8, a, 1e-8)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / S


def add_candidate(arrays, infos, a):
    a = normalize_nonnegative(a)
    if len(a) < 16:
        return
    sc = exact_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    idx = len(arrays)
    arrays.append(a)
    infos.append((float(sc), int(idx), int(len(a))))


def generate_candidates(parent, deadline):
    arrays = []
    infos = []

    add_candidate(arrays, infos, parent)

    lengths = []
    pl = len(parent)
    if 1024 <= pl <= MAX_N:
        lengths.append(pl)
    for nn in [4096, 8192, 16384, MAX_N]:
        duplicate = False
        for mm in lengths:
            if abs(mm - nn) <= 256:
                duplicate = True
                break
        if not duplicate:
            lengths.append(nn)

    clean_lengths = []
    for v in lengths:
        vi = int(v)
        if 1024 <= vi <= MAX_N:
            found = False
            for w in clean_lengths:
                if w == vi:
                    found = True
                    break
            if not found:
                clean_lengths.append(vi)
    clean_lengths.sort()
    lengths = clean_lengths[:6]

    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        a = resample_density(parent, n)
        add_candidate(arrays, infos, a)

        a2 = a
        if n > 1:
            a2 = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
        add_candidate(arrays, infos, a2)

        a3 = smooth_sequence(a)
        add_candidate(arrays, infos, a3)

        if n > 1:
            a4 = smooth_sequence(a2)
            add_candidate(arrays, infos, a4)

    for n in [4096, 16384, MAX_N]:
        for kind in ["uniform", "lin_inc", "lin_dec", "quad_bowl", "gauss_left", "gauss_right", "two_asym"]:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            a = simple_sequence(n, kind)
            add_candidate(arrays, infos, a)

    if len(arrays) == 0:
        add_candidate(arrays, infos, parent)

    infos.sort()
    return arrays, infos


def softmax_x(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(x))
    y = np.exp(x - m)
    s = float(y.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        y = np.zeros_like(x, dtype=np.float64)
        y[int(np.argmax(x))] = 1.0
        return y
    return y / s


def lse_core(x, beta):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.array([], dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64), 0.0

    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        beta = 1.0

    x = x - float(np.mean(x))
    p = softmax_x(x)
    S = float(p.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        S = 1.0
    else:
        p = p / S

    c = fast_conv(p)
    cmax = float(c.max())

    z = beta * (c - cmax)
    z = np.where(z < 0.0, z, 0.0)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    ws = float(ez.sum())
    if ws <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w = ez / ws

    L = cmax + float(np.log(max(ws, 1e-300))) / beta
    F = float(2.0 * n * L / (S * S))

    rev = p[::-1]
    M2 = 3 * n - 2
    nfft2 = _next_pow2(M2)
    RA = np.fft.rfft(rev, nfft2)
    WA = np.fft.rfft(w, nfft2)
    conv_res = np.fft.irfft(RA * WA, nfft2)[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    gL = 2.0 * conv_res

    # Gradient of 2*n*LSE(c)/(sum(p)^2) on the simplex, expressed in log-softmax
    # coordinates. Since softmax keeps sum(p)=1, this is the usable tangent gradient.
    grad_x = p * (2.0 * n * (gL - 2.0 * L))
    grad_x = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_x), grad_x, 0.0)
    grad_x = grad_x - float(np.mean(grad_x))

    return F, grad_x, cmax


def lse_obj_x(x, beta, scale):
    F, grad_x, _ = lse_core(x, beta)
    if not np.isfinite(F):
        return float("inf"), np.zeros(np.asarray(x).shape, dtype=np.float64)
    if scale != 1.0:
        return float(scale) * F, float(scale) * grad_x
    return F, grad_x


def adam_lse_x(x0, beta, lr, deadline, max_steps=None):
    x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), x

    x = x - float(np.mean(x))
    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.999
    eps = 1e-9

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_x = x.copy()

    lr_cur = float(lr)
    max_lr = max(1.0, 10.0 * float(lr))
    min_lr = max(1e-6, 0.05 * float(lr))

    step = 0
    last_improve_step = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if max_steps is not None and step >= max_steps:
            break

        F, grad_x, cmax = lse_core(x, beta)
        if not np.isfinite(F):
            break

        sc = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_x = x.copy()
            last_improve_step = step
            lr_cur = min(max_lr, lr_cur * 1.04)
        elif step - last_improve_step > 300:
            lr_cur = max(min_lr, lr_cur * 0.82)

        step += 1
        if step > 2000000:
            break

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_x)):
            break
        grad_x = grad_x - float(np.mean(grad_x))
        rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(grad_x * grad_x)))
        if rms <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(rms):
            break

        g = grad_x / rms
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * g
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * g * g

        b1_t = b1 ** step
        b2_t = b2 ** step
        m_hat = m / (1.0 - b1_t)
        v_hat = v / (1.0 - b2_t)

        x = x - lr_cur * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + eps)
        x = x - float(np.mean(x))

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            break

        rng = float(np.max(x) - np.min(x))
        if not np.isfinite(rng) or rng > 180.0:
            xc = x - float(np.mean(x))
            if rng > 0.0:
                xc = xc * (150.0 / rng)
            x = xc

    return best_score, best_x


def run_lbfgs_x(x0, beta, deadline):
    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = x0.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), x0

    base_score = exact_score(softmax_x(x0))
    base_x = x0.copy()

    if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1.0:
        return base_score, base_x

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    lse_core(x0, float(beta))
    eval_time = max(1e-4, time.monotonic() - t0)

    remaining = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())
    if remaining <= 2.0:
        return base_score, base_x

    cap = 2500 if n <= 25000 else 1000
    est = int(max(20, min(cap, (remaining * 0.8) / max(eval_time, 1e-4) / 4.0)))
    est = min(est, 6000)

    scale = float(n)
    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_obj_x,
            x0,
            args=(float(beta), scale),
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxiter": max(50, 2 * est),
                "maxfun": est,
                "ftol": 1e-12,
                "gtol": 1e-9,
                "maxls": 30,
            },
        )
        xres = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        xres = base_x

    xres = np.where(np.isfinite(xres), xres, base_x)
    xres = xres - float(np.mean(xres))

    sc = exact_score(softmax_x(xres))
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return base_score, base_x
    return sc, xres


def pair_move_polish(p, c, deadline):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = p.size
    if n <= 1:
        sc = exact_score(p)
        return sc, p

    p = normalize_nonnegative(p)
    c = fast_conv(p)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
    evaluations = 0

    if n <= 8192:
        K = 18
        pair_limit = 260
        max_evals = 5000
    elif n <= 20000:
        K = 14
        pair_limit = 140
        max_evals = 2600
    else:
        K = 8
        pair_limit = 50
        max_evals = 800

    fracs = [0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35, 0.60, 0.90]

    for pass_factor in PAIR_PASS_BETAS:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2.0:
            break
        if evaluations >= max_evals:
            break

        cmax = float(c.max())
        M = max(cmax, 1e-300)
        beta = max(1000.0, float(pass_factor) / M)

        z = beta * (c - cmax)
        z = np.where(z < 0.0, z, 0.0)
        ez = np.exp(z)
        ws = float(ez.sum())
        if ws <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ws):
            w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = ez / ws

        rev = p[::-1]
        M2 = 3 * n - 2
        nfft = _next_pow2(M2)
        conv_res = np.fft.irfft(
            np.fft.rfft(rev, nfft) * np.fft.rfft(w, nfft),
            nfft,
        )[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
        g = 2.0 * conv_res

        src_scores = np.where(p > 1e-14, g, -np.inf)
        src = np.argsort(src_scores)[-K:]
        dst = np.argsort(g)[:K]

        candidates = []
        for ii in src:
            i = int(ii)
            if p[i] <= 1e-14:
                continue
            for jj in dst:
                j = int(jj)
                if i == j:
                    continue
                pot = float((g[i] - g[j]) * p[i])
                if pot > 1e-30:
                    candidates.append((pot, i, j))

        candidates.sort()
        candidates.reverse()

        improved = False
        accepted_in_pass = 0

        for pot, i, j in candidates[:pair_limit]:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2.0 or evaluations >= max_evals:
                break
            if i == j or p[i] <= 1e-16:
                continue

            cmax = float(c.max())
            tol = 1e-12 * max(1.0, cmax)

            best_delta = 0.0
            best_new_c = None
            best_new_max = float("inf")

            for frac in fracs:
                delta = float(frac) * float(p[i])
                if delta <= 1e-18:
                    continue

                new_c = c.copy()
                coeff = 2.0 * delta
                new_c[j:j + n] += coeff * p
                new_c[i:i + n] -= coeff * p
                delta2 = delta * delta
                new_c[2 * j] += delta2
                new_c[i + j] -= 2.0 * delta2
                new_c[2 * i] += delta2

                evaluations += 1
                nm = float(new_c.max())
                if nm < best_new_max:
                    best_new_max = nm
                    best_delta = delta
                    best_new_c = new_c

            if best_delta > 0.0 and best_new_max < cmax - tol:
                c = best_new_c
                p[i] -= best_delta
                p[j] += best_delta
                if p[i] < 1e-15:
                    p[i] = 0.0
                if p[j] < 0.0:
                    p[j] = 0.0
                cmax = best_new_max
                improved = True
                accepted_in_pass += 1

                if accepted_in_pass % 80 == 0:
                    p = np.where(p > 0.0, p, 0.0)
                    S2 = float(p.sum())
                    if S2 > 1e-15:
                        p = p / S2
                    c = fast_conv(p)
                    cmax = float(c.max())

        if not improved:
            accepted_in_pass = 0

    p = normalize_nonnegative(p)
    final_score = exact_score(p)
    return final_score, p


def optimize_candidate(p, deadline):
    start = time.monotonic()
    p = normalize_nonnegative(p)
    n = len(p)

    best_score = exact_score(p)
    best_p = p.copy()

    floor = 1e-12
    p_pos = np.where(p > floor, p, floor)
    S = float(p_pos.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        p_pos = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        S = 1.0
    p_pos = p_pos / S
    x = np.log(p_pos)
    best_x = x.copy()

    total = max(0.0, float(deadline) - start)

    if total > 30.0 and n > 1:
        screen_dl = start + max(5.0, min(40.0, 0.08 * total))
        screen_results = [(best_score, x.copy(), int(min(1.0e8, max(1000.0, 20.0 * float(n)))), 0.05)]

        if total > 60.0:
            ngrid = len(ADAM_GRID)
            screen_each = max(0.5, (screen_dl - time.monotonic()) / max(1, ngrid))
            for bf, lr in ADAM_GRID:
                d = min(screen_dl, time.monotonic() + screen_each)
                if time.monotonic() >= d:
                    break
                beta = int(min(1.0e8, max(1000.0, float(bf) * float(n))))
                sc, xr = adam_lse_x(x, beta, lr, d, max_steps=20000)
                screen_results.append((sc, xr, beta, lr))

            screen_results.sort()

            adam_dl = start + min(0.70 * total, 1500.0)

            if time.monotonic() < adam_dl and len(screen_results) > 0:
                top = screen_results[0]
                rem = adam_dl - time.monotonic()
                if rem > 5.0:
                    d = time.monotonic() + max(5.0, rem * 0.65)
                    sc, xr = adam_lse_x(top[1], top[2], top[3], d)
                    p_new = softmax_x(xr)
                    sc_new = exact_score(p_new)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
                        best_score = sc_new
                        best_p = p_new.copy()
                        best_x = xr.copy()

                if time.monotonic() < adam_dl and len(screen_results) > 1:
                    second = screen_results[1]
                    d = adam_dl
                    sc, xr = adam_lse_x(second[1], second[2], second[3], d)
                    p_new = softmax_x(xr)
                    sc_new = exact_score(p_new)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
                        best_score = sc_new
                        best_p = p_new.copy()
                        best_x = xr.copy()

            lbfgs_dl = start + 0.93 * total
            for idx, fac in enumerate(LBFGS_FACTORS):
                if time.monotonic() >= lbfgs_dl - 3.0:
                    break
                rem = lbfgs_dl - time.monotonic()
                if rem < 10.0:
                    break
                left = len(LBFGS_FACTORS) - idx
                if left == 1:
                    alloc = rem * 0.90
                elif left == 2:
                    alloc = rem * 0.55
                else:
                    alloc = rem * 0.40
                d = time.monotonic() + max(5.0, alloc)
                beta = int(min(1.0e8, max(1000.0, float(fac) * float(n))))
                sc, xr = run_lbfgs_x(best_x, beta, d)
                p_new = softmax_x(xr)
                sc_new = exact_score(p_new)
                if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_new
                    best_p = p_new.copy()
                    best_x = xr.copy()

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
            c = fast_conv(best_p)
            sc_new, p_new = pair_move_polish(best_p, c, deadline)
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
                best_score = sc_new
                best_p = p_new.copy()

    return best_score, best_p


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    base_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 20.0)
    final_deadline = t0 + base_budget

    parent = None
    try:
        raw = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if raw.size >= 16 and np.any(np.isfinite(raw)):
            parent = normalize_nonnegative(raw)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None or len(parent) < 16:
        parent = np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64) / 8192.0

    if len(parent) > MAX_N:
        parent = resample_density(parent, MAX_N)

    par_score = exact_score(parent)
    print(f"Parent: n={len(parent)}, internal score={par_score:.10f}")

    best_a = parent.copy()
    best_score = par_score

    gen_deadline = t0 + min(180.0, 0.18 * base_budget)
    gen_deadline = min(gen_deadline, final_deadline - 30.0)
    arrays, infos = generate_candidates(parent, gen_deadline)

    if len(infos) == 0:
        arrays = [parent.copy()]
        infos = [(par_score, 0, len(parent))]
    else:
        # Choose an initial candidate. Prefer the largest length that is competitive,
        # because longer smooth sequences often have better finite-length slack.
        base_best = infos[0][0]
        first_idx = infos[0][1]
        first_len = infos[0][2]
        for sc, idx, nn in infos:
            if sc <= base_best + 0.005 and nn > first_len:
                first_len = nn
                first_idx = idx

        other_indices = []
        other_lens = [first_len]
        for sc, idx, nn in infos:
            if idx == first_idx:
                continue
            if nn in other_lens:
                continue
            other_indices.append(idx)
            other_lens.append(nn)
            if len(other_indices) >= 2:
                break
        if len(other_indices) < 2:
            used = {first_idx, first_idx}
            for sc, idx, nn in infos:
                if idx in used:
                    continue
                other_indices.append(idx)
                used.add(idx)
                if len(other_indices) >= 2:
                    break

        candidate_order = [first_idx] + other_indices

    reserve = max(180.0, 0.20 * base_budget)
    candidate_end = t0 + base_budget - reserve
    if candidate_end + 10.0 > final_deadline:
        candidate_end = final_deadline - 10.0
    if candidate_end < time.monotonic() + 10.0:
        candidate_end = time.monotonic() + 10.0

    n_candidates = max(1, len(candidate_order))
    for r, idx in enumerate(candidate_order):
        rem = candidate_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 15.0:
            break
        if n_candidates == 1:
            frac = 0.95
        elif n_candidates == 2:
            frac = 0.60 if r == 0 else 0.95
        else:
            if r == 0:
                frac = 0.50
            elif r == 1:
                frac = 0.25
            else:
                frac = 0.95
        alloc = max(10.0, min(rem * frac, 2500.0))
        dl = time.monotonic() + alloc
        if dl > candidate_end:
            dl = candidate_end

        a0 = arrays[idx]
        sc_new, a_new = optimize_candidate(a0, dl)
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
            best_score = sc_new
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            print(f"Candidate idx={idx} n={len(a_new)} improved to {sc_new:.10f}")

    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 120.0 and len(best_a) < MAX_N:
        seed_a = resample_density(best_a, MAX_N)
        sc_seed = exact_score(seed_a)
        if np.isfinite(sc_seed) and sc_seed < best_score:
            best_score = sc_seed
            best_a = seed_a
            print(f"Upscale seed n={len(seed_a)} to {sc_seed:.10f}")

        sc_new, a_new = optimize_candidate(seed_a, final_deadline - 5.0)
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
            best_score = sc_new
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            print(f"Upscale final n={len(a_new)} improved to {sc_new:.10f}")

    if len(best_a) > MAX_N:
        best_a = resample_density(best_a, MAX_N)
        best_score = exact_score(best_a)

    out = []
    for val in best_a:
        f = float(val)
        if not np.isfinite(f):
            f = 0.0
        if f < 0.0:
            f = 0.0
        elif f > 1000.0:
            f = 1000.0
        out.append(f)

    if len(out) == 0 or sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [1.0] * 64

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = float("inf")

    if not np.isfinite(verified) or verified > best_score + 1e-4:
        try:
            verified_parent = float(evaluate_sequence(
                [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, v))) for v in normalize_nonnegative(parent)]
            ))
            if np.isfinite(verified_parent) and verified_parent < verified:
                out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, v))) for v in normalize_nonnegative(parent)]
                verified = verified_parent
        except Exception:
            pass

    print(f"FINAL internal={best_score:.10f}, verified={verified:.10f}, n={len(out)}")
    return out
```
